    Refiners and petrochemical producers with condensate
splitters for naphtha output are heavily relying on Australian,
Malaysian and African ultra-light crudes, after struggling to
secure steady Middle East DFC and LSC supplies since the
Middle East war started in March, feedstock managers at
Japanese, South Korean, Chinese and Thai refiners in Ibaraki,
Incheon, Seosan, Fujian and Rayong, as well as Singapore-
based traders, told Platts, part of S&P Global Energy, in market
discussions Oct. 9-15.

Portland, while New York Harbor was comparatively steady. The
declines coincided with inventory builds on the Gulf Coast and in
the Midwest, even as East and West Coast stocks edged lower.

Dirty tanker freight rates elevated
Resupply fears could tighten market

surrounding resupply emerged amid elevated dirty tanker

                                                                                         BFF                                  DAP Central Europe Eur/CPA Wkly
    Cubic Meter Pure Alcohol (CPA)
    · Type: Volume Unit
    · Definition: A unit of volume that measures the volume of
pure (100%) alcohol. One CBMPA equals 1 cubic meter (1,000
liters) of pure alcohol. Unlike a standard cubic meter (m³), which
measures the total volume of any substance (liquid, gas, or solid),
CBMPA refers specifically to the ethanol content, not the total
liquid volume.
